        "/api/login_check": {
            "post": {
                "tags": [
                    "Authentication"
                ],
                "summary": "Authentification JWT",
                "operationId": "post_api_auth_login",
                "requestBody": {
                    "required": true,
                    "content": {
                        "application/json": {
                            "schema": {
                                "properties": {
                                    "email": {
                                        "type": "string",
                                        "example": "user@vpdive.com"
                                    },
                                    "password": {
                                        "type": "string",
                                        "example": "password123"
                                    }
                                },
                                "type": "object"
                            }
                        }
                    }
                },
                "responses": {
                    "200": {
                        "description": "Returned JWT token",
                        "content": {
                            "application/json": {
                                "schema": {
                                    "properties": {
                                        "token": {
                                            "type": "string",
                                            "example": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1..."
                                        },
                                        "roles": {
                                            "type": "array",
                                            "items": {
                                                "type": "string"
                                            },
                                            "example": [
                                                "ROLE_USER",
                                                "ROLE_ADMIN"
                                            ]
                                        },
                                        "permissions": {
                                            "type": "object",
                                            "example": "{ \"user_view\": true, \"club_edit\": false }"
                                        }
                                    },
                                    "type": "object"
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    "401": {
                        "description": "Invalid logins"
                    }
                }
            }
